Each rank drives one GPU\n// (round-robin over the node-local devices) and keeps its slab plus one ghost\n// plane on each side in device memory. Per iteration the two slab-edge planes\n// are computed first on a dedicated stream and shipped to the neighbours via\n// pinned host buffers (MPI is not assumed to be CUDA-aware) while the interior\n// planes are computed concurrently on a second stream. OpenMP parallelizes the\n// host-side work (result assembly and validation).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                  \\\n    do {                                                                                  \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                            \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                                 \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\nconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 16;\n\n// Initialize local planes [0, nzLocal+2) (including ghosts) with the global formula.\n__global__ void initKernel(Real* __restrict__ grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                           const long long zGlobalOfLocal0, const size_t nzAlloc) {\n    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;\n    const size_t total = planeSize * nzAlloc;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < total;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const long long gz = zGlobalOfLocal0 + (long long)(i / planeSize);\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long long)nz) continue;\n        const size_t gidx = (size_t)gz * planeSize + (i % planeSize);\n        grid[i] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute local planes [zBegin, zEnd). Local plane lz corresponds to global plane\n// lz + zOffset. Global boundary points are copied, interior points averaged.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y)\nstencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n              const int nz, const int zBegin, const int zEnd, const int zOffset) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int z0 = zBegin + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + Z_CHUNK, zEnd);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = (size_t)nx * ny;\n    size_t idx = (size_t)z0 * plane + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n    const bool xyBoundary = (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1);\n\n    if (xyBoundary) {\n        for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z, idx += plane) out[idx] = in[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n\n    Real bottom = in[idx - plane];\n    Real center = in[idx];\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z, idx += plane) {\n        const Real top = in[idx + plane];\n        const int gz = z + zOffset;\n        if (gz == 0 || gz == nz - 1) {\n            out[idx] = center;\n        } else {\n            const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n            const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n            const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n            const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation\n            out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n        }\n        bottom = center;\n        center = top;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void launchStencil(const Real* in, Real* out, int nx, int ny, int nz, int zBegin, int zEnd,\n                          int zOffset, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (zEnd <= zBegin) return;\n    dim3 block(BLOCK_X, BLOCK_Y, 1);\n    dim3 grid((nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X, (ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,\n              (zEnd - zBegin + Z_CHUNK - 1) / Z_CHUNK);\n    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(in, out, nx, ny, nz, zBegin, zEnd, zOffset);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    const Real* g = grid.data();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    int worldRank = 0, worldSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n    const bool root = (worldRank == 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;\n\n    // ---- Domain decomposition along Z (only ranks that get at least one plane) ----\n    const int nActive = (int)std::min<size_t>((size_t)worldSize, std::max<size_t>(nz, 1));\n    const bool active = worldRank < nActive;\n    MPI_Comm comm;\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, active ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, worldRank, &comm);\n\n    std::vector<int> planeCounts(nActive), planeDispls(nActive);\n    for (int r = 0, off = 0; r < nActive; ++r) {\n        planeCounts[r] = (int)(nz / nActive + ((size_t)r < nz % nActive ? 1 : 0));\n        planeDispls[r] = off;\n        off += planeCounts[r];\n    }\n\n    // ---- GPU selection: round-robin over node-local devices ----\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n        int localRank = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n        int nDev = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&nDev));\n        if (nDev < 1) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % nDev));\n    }\n\n    int nzLocal = 0, zStart = 0, up = MPI_PROC_NULL, down = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    Real* hSend = nullptr;  // [lower edge | upper edge]\n    Real* hRecv = nullptr;  // [lower ghost | upper ghost]\n    cudaStream_t sEdge = nullptr, sInner = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t evEdge = nullptr, evInner = nullptr;\n\n    if (active) {\n        int rank = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);\n        nzLocal = planeCounts[rank];\n        zStart = planeDispls[rank];\n        if (rank > 0) down = rank - 1;\n        if (rank < nActive - 1) up = rank + 1;\n\n        const size_t localElems = planeSize * (size_t)(nzLocal + 2);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, localElems * sizeof(Real)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, localElems * sizeof(Real)));\n Each rank drives one GPU\n// (round-robin over the node-local devices) and keeps its slab plus one ghost\n// plane on each side in device memory. Per iteration the two slab-edge planes\n// are computed first on a dedicated stream and shipped to the neighbours via\n// pinned host buffers (MPI is not assumed to be CUDA-aware) while the interior\n// planes are computed concurrently on a second stream. OpenMP parallelizes the\n// host-side work (result assembly and validation).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                  \\\n    do {                                                                                  \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                            \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                                 \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\nconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 16;\n\n// Initialize local planes [0, nzLocal+2) (including ghosts) with the global formula.\n__global__ void initKernel(Real* __restrict__ grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                           const long long zGlobalOfLocal0, const size_t nzAlloc) {\n    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;\n    const size_t total = planeSize * nzAlloc;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < total;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const long long gz = zGlobalOfLocal0 + (long long)(i / planeSize);\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long long)nz) continue;\n        const size_t gidx = (size_t)gz * planeSize + (i % planeSize);\n        grid[i] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute local planes [zBegin, zEnd). Local plane lz corresponds to global plane\n// lz + zOffset. Global boundary points are copied, interior points averaged.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y)\nstencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n              const int nz, const int zBegin, const int zEnd, const int zOffset) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int z0 = zBegin + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + Z_CHUNK, zEnd);\n    if (z0 >= z1) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = (size_t)nx * ny;\n    size_t idx = (size_t)z0 * plane + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n    const bool xyBoundary = (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1);\n\n    if (xyBoundary) {\n        for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z, idx += plane) out[idx] = in[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n\n    Real bottom = in[idx - plane];\n    Real center = in[idx];\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z, idx += plane) {\n        const Real top = in[idx + plane];\n        const int gz = z + zOffset;\n        if (gz == 0 || gz == nz - 1) {\n            out[idx] = center;\n        } else {\n            const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n            const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n            const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n            const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation\n            out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n        }\n        bottom = center;\n        center = top;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void launchStencil(const Real* in, Real* out, int nx, int ny, int nz, int zBegin, int zEnd,\n                          int zOffset, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (zEnd <= zBegin) return;\n    dim3 block(BLOCK_X, BLOCK_Y, 1);\n    dim3 grid((nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X, (ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,\n              (zEnd - zBegin + Z_CHUNK - 1) / Z_CHUNK);\n    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(in, out, nx, ny, nz, zBegin, zEnd, zOffset);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    const Real* g = grid.data();\n\n    // 1. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];\n                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];\n                \n                // Simple averaging stencil\n                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Copy boundary values\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    output[idx] = input[idx];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

User-provided OMP_PROC_BIND/OMP_PLACES\n// take precedence.\nstatic void bindHostThreads(MPI_Comm nodeComm, const int device) {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr) return;\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize);\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    const bool haveMask = sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0;\n    const bool unbound = haveMask && CPU_COUNT(&mask) >= (int)sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n\n    // CPUs local to this rank's GPU\n    cpu_set_t gpuLocal;\n    CPU_ZERO(&gpuLocal);\n    char busId[32] = {0};\n    if (cudaDeviceGetPCIBusId(busId, sizeof(busId), device) == cudaSuccess) {\n        for (char* c = busId; *c; ++c) *c = (char)tolower(*c);\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/bus/pci/devices/%s/local_cpulist\", busId);\n        if (!readCpuList(path, &gpuLocal)) CPU_ZERO(&gpuLocal);\n    }\n    std::vector<cpu_set_t> allLocal(localSize);\n    MPI_Allgather(&gpuLocal, sizeof(cpu_set_t), MPI_BYTE, allLocal.data(), sizeof(cpu_set_t), MPI_BYTE, nodeComm);\n    if (!haveMask) return;\n\n    auto physicalCores = [&](auto inSet) {\n        std::vector<int> cores;\n        for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n            if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &mask) || !inSet(c)) continue;\n            int first = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int a = c;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &a) == 1 && a >= 0 && a < CPU_SETSIZE && CPU_ISSET(a, &mask)) first = a;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            if (first == c) cores.push_back(c);\n        }\n        return cores;\n    };\n    auto share = [](const std::vector<int>& v, int idx, int n) {\n        return std::vector<int>(v.begin() + v.size() * idx / n, v.begin() + v.size() * (idx + 1) / n);\n    };\n\n    std::vector<int> cores;\n    if (!unbound) {\n        cores = physicalCores([](int) { return true; });\n    } else {\n        if (CPU_COUNT(&gpuLocal) > 0) {\n            int idx = 0, n = 0;\n            for (int r = 0; r < localSize; ++r) {\n                if (CPU_EQUAL(&allLocal[r], &gpuLocal)) {\n                    if (r < localRank) ++idx;\n                    ++n;\n                }\n            }\n            cores = share(physicalCores([&](int c) { return CPU_ISSET(c, &gpuLocal) != 0; }), idx, n);\n        }\n        const std::vector<int> spare = physicalCores([&](int c) {\n            for (const auto& s : allLocal)\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &s)) return false;\n            return true;\n        });\n        const std::vector<int> mine = share(spare, localRank, localSize);\n        cores.insert(cores.end(), mine.begin(), mine.end());\n        if (cores.empty()) cores = share(physicalCores([](int) { return true; }), localRank, localSize);\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) omp_set_num_threads((int)cores.size());\n\n    // The calling (master) thread lands on the first, GPU-local core\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int t = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int n = omp_get_num_threads();\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cores[(size_t)t * cores.size() / n], &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nold=s[s.index('    // ---- GPU selection (round-robin'):s.index('    MPI_Datatype planeType;')]\nnew2='''    // ---- GPU selection (round-robin over node-local devices) and host thread placement ----\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n        int localRank = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n        int nDev = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&nDev));\n        if (nDev < 1) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        const int device = localRank % nDev;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        bindHostThreads(nodeComm, device);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n    }\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new2)\ns=s.replace('#include <algorithm>\\n','#include <algorithm>\\n#include <cctype>\\n',1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncmake --build build 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\"; *c; ++c) *c = (char)tolower(*c);\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/bus/pci/devices/%s/local_cpulist\", busId);\n        if (!readCpuList(path, &info[1])) CPU_ZERO(&info[1]);\n    }\n    std::vector<cpu_set_t> all(2 * (size_t)localSize);\n    MPI_Allgather(info, 2 * sizeof(cpu_set_t), MPI_BYTE, all.data(), 2 * sizeof(cpu_set_t), MPI_BYTE, nodeComm);\n    int allHaveMask = 0;\n    MPI_Allreduce(&haveMask, &allHaveMask, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, nodeComm);\n    if (!allHaveMask) return;\n    const cpu_set_t& mask = info[0];\n    const cpu_set_t& gpuLocal = info[1];\n\n    auto physicalCores = [&](auto inSet) {\n        std::vector<int> cores;\n        for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n            if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &mask) || !inSet(c)) continue;\n            int first = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int a = c;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &a) == 1 && a >= 0 && a < CPU_SETSIZE && CPU_ISSET(a, &mask)) first = a;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            if (first == c) cores.push_back(c);\n        }\n        return cores;\n    };\n    auto share = [](const std::vector<int>& v, int idx, int n) {\n        return std::vector<int>(v.begin() + v.size() * idx / n, v.begin() + v.size() * (idx + 1) / n);\n    };\n\n    // Ranks sharing my mask; among them, those sharing my GPU-local CPU set\n    std::vector<int> group;\n    int gIdx = 0, gpuIdx = 0, gpuN = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < localSize; ++r) {\n        if (!CPU_EQUAL(&all[2 * r], &mask)) continue;\n        if (r < localRank) ++gIdx;\n        group.push_back(r);\n        if (CPU_COUNT(&gpuLocal) > 0 && CPU_EQUAL(&all[2 * r + 1], &gpuLocal)) {\n            if (r < localRank) ++gpuIdx;\n            ++gpuN;\n        }\n    }\n    const int gN = (int)group.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> cores;\n    if (gpuN > 0) cores = share(physicalCores([&](int c) { return CPU_ISSET(c, &gpuLocal) != 0; }), gpuIdx, gpuN);\n    const std::vector<int> spare = share(physicalCores([&](int c) {\n                                             for (int r : group)\n                                                 if (CPU_ISSET(c, &all[2 * r + 1])) return false;\n                                             return true;\n                                         }),\n                                         gIdx, gN);\n    cores.insert(cores.end(), spare.begin(), spare.end());\n    if (cores.empty()) cores = share(physicalCores([](int) { return true; }), gIdx, gN);\n    if (cores.empty()) {\n        // Fewer cores than ranks in the mask: at least keep the host threads apart\n        const std::vector<int> pool = physicalCores([](int) { return true; });\n        if (pool.empty()) return;\n        cores.push_back(pool[(size_t)gIdx % pool.size()]);\n    }\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) omp_set_num_threads((int)cores.size());\n\n    // The calling (master) thread lands on the first, GPU-local core\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int t = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int n = omp_get_num_threads();\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cores[(size_t)t * cores.size() / n], &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncmake --build build 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\"; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n            if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &mask) || !inSet(c)) continue;\n            int first = c;\n            char path[128];\n            snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n            if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                int a = c;\n                if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &a) == 1 && a >= 0 && a < CPU_SETSIZE && CPU_ISSET(a, &mask)) first = a;\n                fclose(f);\n            }\n            if (first == c) cores.push_back(c);\n        }\n        return cores;\n    };\n    auto share = [](const std::vector<int>& v, int idx, int n) {\n        return std::vector<int>(v.begin() + v.size() * idx / n, v.begin() + v.size() * (idx + 1) / n);\n    };\n\n    // Ranks sharing my mask; among them, those sharing my GPU-local CPU set\n    std::vector<int> group;\n    int gIdx = 0, gpuIdx = 0, gpuN = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < localSize; ++r) {\n        if (!CPU_EQUAL(&all[2 * r], &mask)) continue;\n        if (r < localRank) ++gIdx;\n        group.push_back(r);\n        if (CPU_COUNT(&gpuLocal) > 0 && CPU_EQUAL(&all[2 * r + 1], &gpuLocal)) {\n            if (r < localRank) ++gpuIdx;\n            ++gpuN;\n        }\n    }\n    const int gN = (int)group.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> cores;\n    if (gpuN > 0) cores = share(physicalCores([&](int c) { return CPU_ISSET(c, &gpuLocal) != 0; }), gpuIdx, gpuN);\n    const std::vector<int> spare = share(physicalCores([&](int c) {\n                                             for (int r : group)\n                                                 if (CPU_ISSET(c, &all[2 * r + 1])) return false;\n                                             return true;\n                                         }),\n                                         gIdx, gN);\n    cores.insert(cores.end(), spare.begin(), spare.end());\n    if (cores.empty()) cores = share(physicalCores([](int) { return true; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfi8GbB5MQiS3nfT4kW3k","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"`stencil3d` now runs as an MPI + CUDA + OpenMP hybrid. Its output is bit-identical to the original serial code in every test case, and on this node (4× RTX 3090, 2× EPYC 7763) it reaches about 98–100 GCUPS on 4 GPUs. Only `stencil3d.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` were changed, and the executable name is the same.\n\n**How it works**\n- **MPI:** the grid is split into slabs along Z, one per rank. Ranks beyond the number of Z planes sit idle. Edge planes are exchanged every sweep. Ranks on the same node write edge planes directly into their neighbour's shared-memory buffer and just send a short \"ready\" message. Ranks on different nodes use normal MPI messages through pinned buffers, since this Open MPI build can't send GPU memory directly.\n- **CUDA:** each rank uses one GPU, preferring one on its own CPU socket. Each sweep computes the two slab-edge planes first on a high-priority stream and sends them while the interior is computed on a second stream.\n- **Division:** the `/ 7.0` is replaced by a multiply-and-correct sequence that gives exactly the same result. A 17-billion-value GPU check found no differences. This matters because these GeForce cards do double-precision math at only 1/64 speed, and the standard division is a long instruction sequence.\n- **OpenMP:** each rank's threads are pinned to cores near its GPU, so ranks don't share cores. Open MPI's default per-socket binding, `--bind-to core` and `--bind-to none` all work. Before the timed loop, a short collective test decides whether the CPU should compute part of each slab alongside the GPU, and that includes GPU-only as an option. OpenMP also speeds up initialization and validation.\n\n**Performance** (512³, 300 iterations)\n\n| Ranks | GCUPS |\n|---|---|\n| Original serial (256³, 10 iterations) | 0.45 |\n| 1 rank, GPU only | 25.9 |\n| 1 rank, GPU + CPU | 33 |\n| 2 ranks | 53 |\n| 4 ranks | 98–100 (1024³: 105) |\n\nFour GPUs reach about 3.8× one GPU. The fixes that got there were stream priorities (np=2 went from 23 to 51), the shared-memory halo exchange, and keeping each rank on its GPU's socket (np=4 went from ~43 to ~98).\n\n**Testing:** I compared the printed results (sum, samples and hash from `-r`) with the original program on 7 grid shapes, from 3×4×9 and nz=2 up to 200×150×301, with 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 and 8 ranks. All matched exactly. I also ran temporary test builds that forced CPU shares (including different shares on neighbouring ranks) and forced the inter-node MPI path; all matched too, and those test hooks have been removed.\n\n**Things to know**\n- I couldn't test across multiple nodes. The inter-node path was only checked by forcing it on one node.\n- With 1 rank under Open MPI's default binding, the rank gets a single core, so the CPU doesn't help (about 26.5 GCUPS). Use `--bind-to none` to get the CPU+GPU result (~33).\n- The calibration happens before the timer starts, so it doesn't affect the reported time, but it adds a little to total runtime.\n- `CMakeLists.txt` now compiles `stencil3d.cpp` as CUDA, requires MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA toolkit, and needs CMake 3.24 or newer for automatic GPU architecture detection."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":754,"cache_read_input_tokens":139573,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":754},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"79d69eb5-5dc4-44c8-aec9-9f38727abef4","uuid":"4b0ac3c3-7c76-4f26-9862-64f424bdd032","timestamp":"2026-10-05T00:15:19.196Z","request_id":"req_011Cfi8GaRBKqXpjm2j2ycqa"}
